# Open-passage section-counting audit

Runtime cap policy: a physiological opening is not a solid end plate. Hollow single-surface conduits use paired private counting walls with annular mouth rims. The original displayed anatomy, textures and coordinates remain unchanged. Closed paired gastric walls count their existing tissue volume directly. Partially visible female gastric regions join only corresponding inner/outer tissue boundaries; all 15 nonempty regional combinations are checked.

The full organ terminology inventories and provisional source/interface limitations remain in TA98-STOMACH-PARTS.md and the relevant TA98 organ reports. This report inventories the runtime pieces affected by the shared cap rule; it does not claim representation of missing wall layers or microscopic descendants. TA98 mappings and parentage below come from the current catalogue concepts and public/assets/ta98.json; unmapped pieces remain explicitly unresolved. The provisional inset is a display approximation, not anatomically verified histology.

Native counting audit: 857 male and 588 female open-surface pieces screened; zero generated fan end-disk triangles. The coarse topology diagnostic flags 34 male and 132 female counting walls for further review. Of these, 28 male and 118 female sources already have duplicate/degenerate/nonmanifold/winding errors; the remaining cases require exact-coordinate replay of possible inset collapse or diagnostic weld artifacts. Zero open edges alone is not acceptance. Full self-intersection and lumen review is incomplete for these generic sources. No malformed source is called repaired.

Independent gastric replay finds zero added-rim self/source intersections in all 15 subsets, with closed tissue boundaries; it does not establish complete anatomical acceptance. Generic live cap behavior is tested for digestive, respiratory, urinary, reproductive, arterial and venous conduits. Source arrays and cached counting geometry are retained between cuts.

Evidence: outputs/open-passage-cap-audit.json; outputs/gastric-cap-subset-evidence.json; outputs/duodenal-port-lumen-probes.json. Reproduce the native counting audit with `node scripts/audit-section-open-passages.mjs`; run `npm run test:sections` for runtime regressions. The audit helper and generated evidence remain intentionally local under the existing ignore rules; the runtime regressions are added to the already tracked section validator. Local assets remain local, and ignore rules are unchanged.
